```typescript
→ 104 │         const filePath: string = path.resolve(req.body.layout).toLowerCase()
  105 │         const isForbiddenFile: boolean = (filePath.includes('ftp') || filePath.includes('ctf.key') || filePath.includes('encryptionkeys'))
  106 │         if (!isForbiddenFile) {
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-217](#m-217) — Stop passing request body into render options

**Classification:** Insecure File Handling · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-73](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/73.html) · [OWASP A06:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A06_2025-Insecure_Design/)

<a id="t-049"></a><a id="f-049"></a>
#### F-049 · Memories endpoint embeds full user records for anonymous

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/memory.ts:24`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-DATA-01](#tc-data-01))

**Reliability:** Medium. An automatic check confirmed part of the claim.

**Issue:** An anonymous caller requests GET `/rest/memories`. The handler includes the User model with no attribute selection, so the response can carry each poster's email, password hash, role, `deluxeToken` and `totpSecret`.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  22 │ export function getMemories () {
  23 │   return async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
→ 24 │     const memories = await MemoryModel.findAll({ include: [UserModel] })
  25 │     res.status(200).json({ status: 'success', data: memories })
  26 │   }
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-218](#m-218) — Restrict included user attributes

**Classification:** Error Information Disclosure · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-213](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/213.html) · [OWASP A02:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A02_2025-Security_Misconfiguration/) · Verification items: [TC-DATA-01](#tc-data-01)

<a id="t-050"></a><a id="f-050"></a>
#### F-050 · JSONP whoami exposes selectable user fields

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/currentUser.ts:58`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** `/rest/user/whoami` authenticates through the token cookie, returns any `user.data` field named in ?fields (including the password hash), and answers with JSONP when ?callback is set. A malicious page can load it as a script in the victim's browser and read the victim's email and password hash, provided the cookie is sent cross-site.

SameSite was not verified.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  56 │     } else {
  57 │       challengeUtils.solveIf(challenges.emailLeakChallenge, () => { return true })
→ 58 │       res.jsonp(response)
  59 │     }
  60 │   }
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-219](#m-219) — Restrict CORS to trusted origins

**Classification:** Supply-Chain Integrity · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-346](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/346.html) · [OWASP A03:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A03_2025-Software_Supply_Chain_Failures/)

<a id="t-051"></a><a id="f-051"></a>
#### F-051 · Memories endpoint returns full associated user records

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/memory.ts:24`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-034](#w-034) - Memories endpoint returns full associated user records (`memory.ts:24`)

**Issue:** An unauthenticated client calls GET `/rest/memories`; `getMemories` includes the full UserModel for each memory with no attribute filter, potentially exposing emails, password hashes (`MD5`), `totpSecret` and `deluxeToken`. Whether a default scope strips fields was not visible.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  22 │ export function getMemories () {
  23 │   return async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
→ 24 │     const memories = await MemoryModel.findAll({ include: [UserModel] })
  25 │     res.status(200).json({ status: 'success', data: memories })
  26 │   }
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-218](#m-218) — Restrict included user attributes

**Classification:** Error Information Disclosure · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-359](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/359.html) · [OWASP A02:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A02_2025-Security_Misconfiguration/)

<a id="t-052"></a><a id="f-052"></a>
#### F-052 · Change password sends current and new passwords

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/changePassword.ts:14`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-DATA-04](#tc-data-04))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-036](#w-036) - Change password sends current and new passwords in URL query

**Issue:** GET `/rest/user/change-password` reads the current, new and repeat passwords from the query string. The passwords then land in access logs, proxy logs and browser history, where other parties can read them.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  12 │ export function changePassword () {
  13 │   return async ({ query, headers, connection }: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
→ 14 │     const currentPassword = query.current as string
  15 │     const newPassword = query.new as string
  16 │     const newPasswordInString = newPassword?.toString()
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-220](#m-220) — Move password change to POST request body

**Classification:** Cryptographic Failures · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-598](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/598.html) · [OWASP A04:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A04_2025-Cryptographic_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-DATA-04](#tc-data-04)

<a id="t-053"></a><a id="f-053"></a>
#### F-053 · XML external entity resolution in upload parser

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `lib/xml.ts:35`

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: Parser is XXE-enabled, but the `/file-upload` route, `handleXmlUpload` and the 400-char echo are not in supplied source.

**Weakness:** [W-020](#w-020) - XML external entity resolution in upload parser (`xml.ts:35`)

**Issue:** An unauthenticated attacker uploads an .xml file to `/file-upload` declaring an external entity `file:///etc/passwd`. `parseXmlString` enables NOENT and DTDLOAD with filesystem input providers, and `handleXmlUpload` echoes up to 400 chars of the parsed document in the error response.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

```typescript
  33 │ export async function parseXmlString (data: string, timeoutMs = 2000): Promise<string> {
  34 │   const libxml2 = await loadLibxml2()
→ 35 │   const option = libxml2.ParseOption.XML_PARSE_NOENT | libxml2.ParseOption.XML_PARSE_DTDLOAD | libxml2.ParseOption.XML_PARSE_NOBLANKS | libxml2.ParseOption.XML_PARSE_NOCDATA
  36 │   const sandbox = { libxml2, data, option }
  37 │   vm.createContext(sandbox)
```

**Fix:** Disable external entity resolution on every XML parser and reject DOCTYPE declarations → ◑ [M-170](#m-170) — Manual review: verify XML external entity resolution in upload parser · ◕ [M-221](#m-221) — Disable XML external entity (XXE) resolution

**Classification:** Insecure File Handling · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-611](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/611.html) · [OWASP A06:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A06_2025-Insecure_Design/)

<a id="t-054"></a><a id="f-054"></a>
#### F-054 · XML parser enables external entities and DTD loading

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `lib/xml.ts:35`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-INPUT-06](#tc-input-06))

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Weakness:** [W-021](#w-021) - XML parser enables external entities and DTD loading (`xml.ts:35`)

**Issue:** At `lib/xml.ts:35`, `parseXmlString` sets `XML_PARSE_NOENT` and `XML_PARSE_DTDLOAD` after registering host filesystem input providers, so external entities in XML can read local files such as `/etc/passwd`. The parts differ on the entry point. A 2 s vm timeout limits only entity-expansion time.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  33 │ export async function parseXmlString (data: string, timeoutMs = 2000): Promise<string> {
  34 │   const libxml2 = await loadLibxml2()
→ 35 │   const option = libxml2.ParseOption.XML_PARSE_NOENT | libxml2.ParseOption.XML_PARSE_DTDLOAD | libxml2.ParseOption.XML_PARSE_NOBLANKS | libxml2.ParseOption.XML_PARSE_NOCDATA
  36 │   const sandbox = { libxml2, data, option }
  37 │   vm.createContext(sandbox)
```

**Fix:** Disable external entity resolution on every XML parser and reject DOCTYPE declarations → ◕ [M-221](#m-221) — Disable XML external entity (XXE) resolution

**Classification:** Insecure File Handling · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-611](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/611.html) · [OWASP A06:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A06_2025-Insecure_Design/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-06](#tc-input-06)

<a id="t-055"></a><a id="f-055"></a>
#### F-055 · Basket retrieval by id without ownership check

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/basket.ts:19`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01), [TC-DATA-01](#tc-data-01))

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Weakness:** [W-005](#w-005) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** An attacker logs in as any customer to get a valid JWT and calls GET `/rest/basket/`&lt;other id&gt;, for example `/rest/basket/2`. At `routes/basket.ts:19`, the handler loads the basket by the path id alone and returns its products.

`isAuthorized` and `appendUserId` on `/rest/basket` (`server.ts:356`) only check that the caller is logged in.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  17 │     try {
  18 │       const id = req.params.id
→ 19 │       const basket = await BasketModel.findOne({ where: { id }, include: [{ model: ProductModel, paranoid: false, as: 'Products' }] })
  20 │       /* jshint eqeqeq:false */
  21 │       challengeUtils.solveIf(challenges.basketAccessChallenge, () => {
```

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◕ [M-222](#m-222) — Scope basket lookup to the authenticated user

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01), [TC-DATA-01](#tc-data-01)

<a id="t-056"></a><a id="f-056"></a>
#### F-056 · Passwords hashed and compared as unsalted MD5

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/changePassword.ts:39`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHN-04](#tc-authn-04))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-041](#w-041) - Security-sensitive data uses weak cryptographic primitives

**Issue:** `changePassword` compares the current password using `security.hash`, which is unsalted `MD5` (`insecurity.ts:41`). The User model's password setter (`user.ts:76`) stores every password the same way.

An attacker who obtains the Users table, for example through an injection bug, can crack most passwords quickly with precomputed tables or GPU brute force.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - A non-iterating hash is used for password storage.

**Fix:** Replace the broken hash with a salted password-hashing function (bcrypt/Argon2id) → ◕ [M-223](#m-223) — Store passwords with bcrypt or argon2id

**Classification:** Cryptographic Failures · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-916](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/916.html) · [OWASP A04:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A04_2025-Cryptographic_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHN-04](#tc-authn-04)

<a id="t-057"></a><a id="f-057"></a>
#### F-057 · Server-side request forgery in profile

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/profileImageUrlUpload.ts:24`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-INPUT-05](#tc-input-05))

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Trust boundary gap:** [tb-8](#tb-8) - Juice Shop Express API → external · Egress destination: The fetch at the boundary sends requests to a host the requester chooses, with no destination restriction, which breaks the egress-destination assumption.

**Issue:** An authenticated user posts `imageUrl` to POST `/profile/image/url`. At `routes/profileImageUrlUpload.ts:24`, the handler passes the URL to `fetch()` without checking the scheme, host or IP address, and stores the response body as the user's profile image under a public uploads path.

The file extension is restricted, but the content is not.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  22 │       if (loggedInUser) {
  23 │         try {
→ 24 │           const response = await fetch(url)
  25 │           if (!response.ok || !response.body) {
  26 │             throw new Error('url returned a non-OK status code or an empty body')
```

**Fix:** Validate the URL scheme + host against an explicit allow-list before issuing outbound requests → ◕ [M-224](#m-224) — Validate and allowlist outbound request targets

**Classification:** Server-Side Request Forgery · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-918](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/918.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-05](#tc-input-05)

<a id="t-059"></a><a id="f-059"></a>
#### F-059 · Session JWT and 2FA token stored in localStorage and script-readable cookie

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Angular Client Application  ·  **Location:** Multiple locations (2)

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-DATA-02](#tc-data-02))

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Weakness:** [W-009](#w-009) - Browser scripts retain reusable session credentials

**Instances (2):** `frontend/src/app/login/login.component.ts`: 101; `frontend/src/app/two-factor-auth-enter/two-factor-auth-enter.component.ts`: 53

**Issue:** After login, the client writes the bearer JWT to `localStorage` and to a cookie set from script. Because script sets the cookie, it cannot be HttpOnly, and no Secure or SameSite options are passed. This finding holds 2 places in total, so there are further places beyond the one cited here.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - `login.component.ts:101` calls `localStorage.setItem('token')`, line 104 calls `cookieService.put('token')`, and line 120 stores `totp_tmp_token`. `oauth.component.ts:50-51` does the same. `request.interceptor.ts:16` reads the token for the `Authorization` header.

**Fix:** ◕ [M-226](#m-226) — Store session tokens in HttpOnly, Secure cookies

**Classification:** Insecure Client-Side Storage · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-922](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/922.html) · [OWASP A04:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A04_2025-Cryptographic_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-DATA-02](#tc-data-02)

<a id="t-062"></a><a id="f-062"></a>
#### F-062 · B2B order lines evaluated by JavaScript interpreter

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/b2bOrder.ts:23`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-INPUT-03](#tc-input-03))

**Reliability:** Medium. An automatic check confirmed part of the claim.

**Weakness:** [W-001](#w-001) - Application data crosses into executable code

**Issue:** The B2B order handler passes `orderLinesData` from the request body to the notevil evaluator inside a node vm context. Node's vm is not a security boundary, so a caller who escapes the evaluator could run code on the server. Long-running input also ties up the process.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - `body.orderLinesData` (line 19) is evaluated at line 23. The served source does not show whether the evaluator can be escaped.

```typescript
  21 │         const sandbox = { safeEval, orderLinesData }
  22 │         vm.createContext(sandbox)
→ 23 │         vm.runInContext('safeEval(orderLinesData)', sandbox, { timeout: 2000 })
  24 │         res.json({ cid: body.cid, orderNo: uniqueOrderNumber(), paymentDue: dateTwoWeeksFromNow() })
  25 │       } catch (err) {
```

**Fix:** Replace runtime code generation (eval/Function/template render) with a data-only execution path → ◕ [M-229](#m-229) — Remove server-side evaluation of untrusted input (🟠 [F-062](#f-062))

**Classification:** Code Execution via Unsafe Deserialization or Eval · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-94](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/94.html) · [OWASP A08:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A08_2025-Software_or_Data_Integrity_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-03](#tc-input-03)

<a id="t-063"></a><a id="f-063"></a>
#### F-063 · Basket item creation bypasses basket ownership

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/basketItems.ts:37`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-005](#w-005) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** An authenticated user sends POST `/api/BasketItems` with a raw JSON body that contains BasketId twice. The first value is the user's own basket id and the last is a victim's basket id.

The ownership check compares only `basketIds[0]` with the caller's bid. Any logged-in user can add products to other users' baskets, which tampers with their orders and checkout totals.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - `basketItems.ts:37` compares `user.bid` with `basketIds[0]`. Line 42 builds the item with `basketIds[basketIds.length - 1]`, and line 47 saves it.

```typescript
  35 │
  36 │     const user = security.authenticatedUsers.from(req)
→ 37 │     if (user && basketIds[0] && basketIds[0] !== 'undefined' && Number(user.bid) != Number(basketIds[0])) { // eslint-disable-line eqeqeq
  38 │       res.status(401).send('{\'error\' : \'Invalid BasketId\'}')
  39 │     } else {
```

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◕ [M-230](#m-230) — Bind basket items to the caller's own basket

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01)

<a id="t-064"></a><a id="f-064"></a>
#### F-064 · Any authenticated user can rewrite other

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/updateProductReviews.ts:18`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-005](#w-005) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** A logged-in attacker sends PATCH `/rest/products/reviews` with another user's review id and a new message. The handler updates the review by the body id alone (`multi:true`) and never compares the author with the session user.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  16 │     const user = security.authenticatedUsers.from(req) // vuln-code-snippet vuln-line forgedReviewChallenge
  17 │     db.reviewsCollection.update( // vuln-code-snippet neutral-line forgedReviewChallenge
→ 18 │       { _id: req.body.id }, // vuln-code-snippet vuln-line noSqlReviewsChallenge forgedReviewChallenge
  19 │       { $set: { message: req.body.message } },
  20 │       { multi: true } // vuln-code-snippet vuln-line noSqlReviewsChallenge
```

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◕ [M-231](#m-231) — Bind review updates to the authenticated author

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01)

<a id="t-065"></a><a id="f-065"></a>
#### F-065 · Server access logs downloadable without authentication or role

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:283`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-005](#w-005) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** Any anonymous caller can browse `/support/logs` and request `/support/logs/``&lt;file&gt;`. The handler sends any file from the logs directory, including `access.log` files that record other users' IPs and request URLs, and no `isAuthorized` or admin role check is registered on the route.

Operational log data about all users' requests is exposed to unauthenticated callers. This is an administrator-only operation.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - `server.ts:281-283` registers `serveIndex` and `serveLogFiles` for `/support/logs` with no guard, and `logfileServer.ts:14` sends logs/`<file>`.

```typescript
  281 │   app.use('/support/logs', serveIndexMiddleware, serveIndex('logs', { icons: true, view: 'details' })) // vuln-code-snippet vuln-line accessLogDisclosureChallenge
  282 │   app.use('/support/logs', verify.accessControlChallenges()) // vuln-code-snippet hide-line
→ 283 │   app.use('/support/logs/:file', serveLogFiles()) // vuln-code-snippet vuln-line accessLogDisclosureChallenge
  284 │
  285 │   /* Swagger documentation for B2B v2 endpoints */
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-232](#m-232) — Restrict log file access to administrators

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-862](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/862.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02)

<a id="t-066"></a><a id="f-066"></a>
#### F-066 · Sensitive Routes Registered Without Authentication Middleware

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** Multiple locations (4)

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: Route has no middleware auth, but the `profileImageFileUpload` handler (which may check the cookie) was not in the supplied source, so enforcement is unknown.

**Weakness:** [W-005](#w-005) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Instances (4):** `server.ts`: 310, 311, 641, 667

**Issue:** The route registration carries no authentication marker, so an anonymous attacker may call this state-changing operation on a sensitive resource directly, unless a downstream check the scanner cannot see stops it.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

```typescript
server.ts
  308 │   /* File Upload */

  311 │   app.post('/profile/image/url', uploadToMemory.single('file'), utils.asyncHandler(profileImageUrlUpload()))
```

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-862](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/862.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHN-01](#tc-authn-01) · Rules: AUTHZ-008

<a id="t-068"></a><a id="f-068"></a>
#### F-068 · Unauthenticated product modification

**Severity:** 🟠 High - verified attack-chain keystone in AC-B-007 (User misuses a privileged function for another purpose); see [§9](#9-abuse-cases)  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:501`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02), [TC-AUTHZ-03](#tc-authz-03))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-005](#w-005) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** An anonymous caller sends PUT `/api/Products/:id` with a new price or description. Finale updates the product because the `isAuthorized` guard for this route is commented out and no role check exists, so the catalog prices and content that every customer sees are tampered with.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - `server.ts` registers guards for POST and DELETE on Products only. The generated PUT endpoint binds price, `deluxePrice` and description from the body with no attribute allow-list.

```typescript
  499 │
  500 │   for (const { name, exclude, model, include } of autoModels) {
→ 501 │     const resource = finale.resource({
  502 │       model,
  503 │       endpoints: [`/api/${name}s`, `/api/${name}s/:id`],
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-234](#m-234) — Restrict product updates to an administrator role

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-862](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/862.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02), [TC-AUTHZ-03](#tc-authz-03)

<a id="t-069"></a><a id="f-069"></a>
#### F-069 · Missing authorization on generated product write endpoints

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:503`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-005](#w-005) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** `finale-rest` generates CRUD endpoints for `/api/Products` and `/api/Products/:id`. The PUT guard is commented out at line 370, so an unauthenticated attacker can send PUT `/api/Products/:id` to change any product's name, description or price. Neither route has an admin role check.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  501 │     const resource = finale.resource({
  502 │       model,
→ 503 │       endpoints: [`/api/${name}s`, `/api/${name}s/:id`],
  504 │       excludeAttributes: exclude,
  505 │       pagination: false,
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-235](#m-235) — Enforce admin role checks on product write routes

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-862](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/862.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/)

<a id="t-070"></a><a id="f-070"></a>
#### F-070 · Chatbot coupon tool mints any discount

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/chat.ts:184`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02))

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Weakness:** [W-005](#w-005) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** At `routes/chat.ts:184`, the schema of the `generateCoupon` tool accepts any number, and execute signs a coupon with `security.generateCoupon(discount)`. POST `/rest/chat` has no authentication or authorization guard. Coupon issuance should be reserved for privileged staff.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  182 │           challengeUtils.solveIf(challenges.chatbotPromptInjectionChallenge, () => discount >= 10) // vuln-code-snippet hide-line
  183 │           challengeUtils.solveIf(challenges.chatbotGreedyInjectionChallenge, () => discount >= 50) // vuln-code-snippet hide-line
→ 184 │           const couponCode = security.generateCoupon(discount) // vuln-code-snippet vuln-line chatbotPromptInjectionChallenge
  185 │           return { couponCode, discount } // vuln-code-snippet neutral-line chatbotPromptInjectionChallenge
  186 │         }
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-236](#m-236) — Enforce discount limits and eligibility in tool code

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-863](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/863.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02)

<a id="t-071"></a><a id="f-071"></a>
#### F-071 · Self-registration accepts a client-supplied admin role

**Severity:** 🟠 High - elevated to Critical: reaches a privileged operation on an unauthenticated endpoint  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:408`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-03](#tc-authz-03))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-006](#w-006) - Privileged attributes lack an enforced write allowlist

**Issue:** An unauthenticated attacker posts to `/api/Users` with {email, password, `passwordRepeat`, role: 'admin'}. The finale-generated create binds the whole body to the User model.

The model's role validator allows admin, so the account is created as an administrator and the attacker logs in with admin rights.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - `server.ts:408-422` pre-process POST `/api/Users` without stripping role. `server.ts:501` creates the User finale resource. `models/user.ts:83` allows 'admin' in the role validator, and line 97 stores the value.

```typescript
  406 │   app.post('/api/Feedbacks', verify.captchaBypassChallenge())
  407 │   /* User registration challenge verifications before finale takes over */
→ 408 │   app.post('/api/Users', (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  409 │     if (req.body.email !== undefined && req.body.password !== undefined && req.body.passwordRepeat !== undefined) {
  410 │       if (req.body.email.length !== 0 && req.body.password.length !== 0) {
```

**Fix:** ● [M-237](#m-237) — Strip privileged fields from registration requests

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-915](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/915.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-03](#tc-authz-03) · walkthrough [Walkthrough §3.8](#38-self-registration-accepts-a-client-supplied-admin-role)

```typescript
  180 │         }),
  181 │         execute: async ({ discount }) => {
```

<a id="t-074"></a><a id="f-074"></a>
#### F-074 · Server-side template injection

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/userProfile.ts:87`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-INPUT-03](#tc-input-03))

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Weakness:** [W-018](#w-018) - Application data crosses into template source

**Issue:** At `routes/userProfile.ts:87` the profile handler uses string replace to put the stored username into the Pug template source, then passes the result to `pug.compile()`. Only a single leading backslash is added to the username (lines 63 and 66, on the non-eval path).

The survey also reports that the user-controlled `profileImage` value is concatenated into the `Content-Security-Policy` header (line 88), so a user can add 'unsafe-inline' and weaken the policy.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - Lines 73 and 87 build the Pug source from the stored username and compile it. views/`userProfile.pug` was not read, so the exact injection context is unproven.

```typescript
  85 │     try {
  86 │       const pug = (await import('pug')).default
→ 87 │       const fn = pug.compile(template)
  88 │       const CSP = `img-src 'self' ${user?.profileImage}; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-eval'`
  89 │
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-240](#m-240) — Remove server-side evaluation of untrusted input (🟠 [F-074](#f-074))

**Classification:** Injection · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-1336](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1336.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-03](#tc-input-03)

<a id="t-075"></a><a id="f-075"></a>
#### F-075 · Chatbot coupon tool issues arbitrary discounts on model

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/chat.ts:184`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AI-01](#tc-ai-01), [TC-AI-02](#tc-ai-02), [TC-AI-03](#tc-ai-03))

**Reliability:** Medium. An automatic check confirmed part of the claim.

**Issue:** An unauthenticated caller posts to `/rest/chat` with a crafted messages array, for example injected instructions or fabricated assistant/system turns. The model calls `generateCoupon` with discount 50 or more.

The server mints a valid coupon of that value without checking it, and the caller redeems it at checkout. Attackers can obtain coupons for arbitrary discounts, causing direct financial loss on orders.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - `generateCoupon` accepts an unbounded `z.number()` discount and calls `security.generateCoupon` directly. Client-controlled messages reach the model on an unauthenticated route.

```typescript
  182 │           challengeUtils.solveIf(challenges.chatbotPromptInjectionChallenge, () => discount >= 10) // vuln-code-snippet hide-line
  183 │           challengeUtils.solveIf(challenges.chatbotGreedyInjectionChallenge, () => discount >= 50) // vuln-code-snippet hide-line
→ 184 │           const couponCode = security.generateCoupon(discount) // vuln-code-snippet vuln-line chatbotPromptInjectionChallenge
  185 │           return { couponCode, discount } // vuln-code-snippet neutral-line chatbotPromptInjectionChallenge
  186 │         }
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-241](#m-241) — Enforce coupon policy server-side

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-1427](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1427.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AI-01](#tc-ai-01), [TC-AI-02](#tc-ai-02), [TC-AI-03](#tc-ai-03)

<a id="t-076"></a><a id="f-076"></a>
#### F-076 · JWT verification does not pin the expected algorithm

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-09](#c-09) - Authentication & Session Surface  ·  **Location:** Multiple locations (3)

**Found by:** survey and scanners

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: Two-argument `jws.verify` is cast; real behavior and exploitability depend on the unread jws version.

**Weakness:** [W-003](#w-003) - Authentication relies on hand-rolled token verification

**Instances (3):** `lib/insecurity.ts`: 55, 53, 189

**Issue:** At `lib/insecurity.ts:55`, `verify()` calls `jws.verify(token, publicKey)` with two arguments. In jws versions with that signature, the token header chooses the algorithm. The survey part notes that exploitation depends on the installed jws version, which was not read.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

```typescript
lib/insecurity.ts
  53 │ export const denyAll = () => expressJwt({ secret: '' + Math.random() } as any)
  54 │ export const authorize = (user = {}) => jwt.sign(user, privateKey, { expiresIn: '6h', algorithm: 'RS256' })
→ 55 │ export const verify = (token: string) => token ? (jws.verify as ((token: string, secret: string) => boolean))(token, publicKey) : false
  56 │ export const decode = (token: string) => { return jws.decode(token)?.payload }
  57 │
```

**Fix:** Pin the signature algorithm explicitly and reject `alg:none` and unknown algorithms → ◑ [M-173](#m-173) — Manual review: verify JWT verification does not pin the expected algorithm · ◕ [M-242](#m-242) — Verify tokens with jsonwebtoken pinned to RS256

**Classification:** Broken Authentication · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-347](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/347.html) · [OWASP A07:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A07_2025-Authentication_Failures/)

<a id="t-060"></a><a id="f-060"></a>
#### F-060 · Any authenticated user can list all users'

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-09](#c-09) - Authentication & Session Surface  ·  **Location:** `routes/authenticatedUsers.ts:25`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-DATA-01](#tc-data-01))

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Issue:** Any registered user, including a low-privilege customer, calls GET `/rest/user/authentication-details` with their own JWT. The handler spreads the `dataValues` of every user from `UserModel.findAll()` into the response.

The route's only guard is the `isAuthorized()` JWT check at `server.ts:398`, which has no role check.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - Sensitive runtime values are served unauthenticated to any caller.

**Fix:** Restrict the response to the minimum fields needed and never echo secrets → ◕ [M-227](#m-227) — Restrict and field-select the authentication details response

**Classification:** Error Information Disclosure · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-200](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/200.html) · [OWASP A02:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A02_2025-Security_Misconfiguration/) · Verification items: [TC-DATA-01](#tc-data-01)

Self-registration as an administrator would give full control over application data and admin functions.

<a id="t-023"></a><a id="f-023"></a>
#### F-023 · Product description stored without HTML sanitization

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-01](#c-01) - Sequelize application data access  ·  **Location:** `models/product.ts:45`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** When `restfulXssChallenge` is enabled, anyone able to update a product can save a description containing an iframe or script payload. The setter stores it raw, and every visitor's browser may execute it when the description is rendered.

Stored script in the product catalogue could run in shoppers' sessions and steal tokens or perform actions on their behalf.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - At `models/product.ts:45-55`, the description setter skips sanitization when `restfulXssChallenge` is enabled and calls `setDataValue` with the raw input. The write route and browser rendering sink were not in the served sources.

```typescript
  43 │         type: DataTypes.STRING,
  44 │         set (description: string) {
→ 45 │           if (utils.isChallengeEnabled(challenges.restfulXssChallenge)) {
  46 │             challengeUtils.solveIf(challenges.restfulXssChallenge, () => {
  47 │               return utils.contains(
```

**Fix:** Output-encode untrusted strings at every sink and remove all `bypassSecurityTrustHtml` calls → ◕ [M-195](#m-195) — Sanitize product descriptions in every setter branch

**Classification:** Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/)

#### F-037 · Stored XSS

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Angular Client Application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/about/about.component.html:51`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-INPUT-04](#tc-input-04))

**Reliability:** Medium. An automatic check confirmed part of the claim.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** An attacker submits feedback through POST `/api/Feedbacks` with an HTML payload in the comment. When any visitor opens the About page, the client interpolates the comment into an HTML string, marks it as trusted and binds it to [`innerHTML`].

The payload runs in the visitor's browser and can read the JWT stored in `localStorage`.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```html
  49 │           <ng-container *galleryImageDef="let item; let active = active">
  50 │             @if (active) {
→ 51 │               <figure class="feedback" [innerHTML]="item?.args"></figure>
  52 │             }
  53 │           </ng-container>
```

**Fix:** Output-encode untrusted strings at every sink and remove all `bypassSecurityTrustHtml` calls → ◕ [M-206](#m-206) — Stop trusting feedback HTML and bind as text

**Classification:** Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-04](#tc-input-04)

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Angular Client Application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/about/about.component.ts:119`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** An attacker submits feedback through POST `/api/Feedbacks` with an HTML or script payload in the comment. The About page places each comment inside an HTML string, marks it trusted with DomSanitizer, and renders it through [`innerHTML`].

Script then runs in every visitor's browser and can read the JWT from `localStorage`.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - `about.component.ts:116-121` interpolates feedbacks[i].comment into HTML and passes it to `bypassSecurityTrustHtml`. `about.component.html:51` renders `item.args` via [`innerHTML`]. The input comes from `feedback.service.ts:27`. Whether the server sanitizes comments before storage was not inspected.

```typescript
  117 │             feedbacks[i].comment
  118 │           }</p><div class="feedback-stars">(${this.stars[feedbacks[i].rating]})</div></figcaption>`
→ 119 │           feedbacks[i].comment = this.sanitizer.bypassSecurityTrustHtml(
  120 │             feedbacks[i].comment
  121 │           )
```

**Fix:** Output-encode untrusted strings at every sink and remove all `bypassSecurityTrustHtml` calls → ◕ [M-207](#m-207) — Encode output instead of bypassing the framework sanitizer (🟠 [F-038](#f-038))

**Classification:** Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/)

<a id="t-041"></a><a id="f-041"></a>
#### F-041 · Stored XSS

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Angular Client Application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/administration/administration.component.ts:73`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** An attacker registers through POST `/api/Users` with an email value containing HTML markup. `findAllUsers` interpolates `user.email` into an HTML string and marks it trusted with `bypassSecurityTrustHtml`.

The admin user table then binds it to [`innerHTML`], so the markup runs when an administrator views the user list. Script runs in the administrator's browser and can steal the admin JWT.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - `administration.component.ts:73` builds `<span ...>${user.email}</span>` and passes it to `bypassSecurityTrustHtml`. `administration.component.html:26` and :113 render it with [`innerHTML`]. Server-side email validation and sanitization were not inspected.

```typescript
  71 │         this.userDataSourceHidden = users
  72 │         for (const user of this.userDataSource) {
→ 73 │           user.email = this.sanitizer.bypassSecurityTrustHtml(`<span class="${this.doesUserHaveAnActiveSession(user) ? 'confirmation' : 'error'}">${user.email}</span>`)
  74 │         }
  75 │         this.userDataSource = new MatTableDataSource(this.userDataSource)
```

**Fix:** Output-encode untrusted strings at every sink and remove all `bypassSecurityTrustHtml` calls → ◕ [M-210](#m-210) — Encode output instead of bypassing the framework sanitizer (🟠 [F-041](#f-041))

**Classification:** Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/)

<a id="t-042"></a><a id="f-042"></a>
#### F-042 · Stored XSS

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Angular Client Application  ·  **Location:** Multiple locations (2)

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Instances (2):** `frontend/src/app/administration/administration.component.ts`: 91; `frontend/src/app/feedback-details/feedback-details.component.html`: 18

**Issue:** An attacker posts a feedback comment containing HTML or JavaScript. POST `/api/Feedbacks` is open without login, guarded only by the captcha and challenge middleware.

When an administrator opens /#`/administration`, `findAllFeedbacks` wraps each comment in `bypassSecurityTrustHtml`, and the template binds it to [`innerHTML`]. Script runs in an administrator session, enabling theft of the admin token and admin actions.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified - `administration.component.ts:91` marks `feedback.comment` as trusted HTML, and `administration.component.html:60` renders it with [`innerHTML`]. Any server-side sanitization of stored comments was not inspected.

```typescript
frontend/src/app/administration/administration.component.ts
  89 │         this.feedbackDataSource = feedbacks
  90 │         for (const feedback of this.feedbackDataSource) {
→ 91 │           feedback.comment = this.sanitizer.bypassSecurityTrustHtml(feedback.comment)
  92 │         }
  93 │         this.feedbackDataSource = new MatTableDataSource(this.feedbackDataSource)
```

**Fix:** Output-encode untrusted strings at every sink and remove all `bypassSecurityTrustHtml` calls → ◕ [M-211](#m-211) — Remove bypassSecurityTrustHtml and render comments as text

**Classification:** Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/)

<a id="t-043"></a><a id="f-043"></a>
#### F-043 · DOM XSS

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Angular Client Application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/search-result/search-result.component.html:11`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-INPUT-04](#tc-input-04))

**Reliability:** Medium. An automatic check confirmed part of the claim.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** An attacker sends a victim a link such as /#`/search?q=&lt`;iframe `src=javascript`:`alert(document.cookie)`&gt;. The client reads the q parameter, marks it as trusted HTML and renders it through [`innerHTML`] in the search heading.

The script runs in the victim's session and can steal the JWT stored in `localStorage`.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```html
   9 │       <div>
  10 │         <span>{{ "TITLE_SEARCH_RESULTS" | translate }} - </span>
→ 11 │         <span id="searchValue" [innerHTML]="searchValue"></span>
  12 │       </div>
  13 │     } @else {
```

**Fix:** Output-encode untrusted strings at every sink and remove all `bypassSecurityTrustHtml` calls → ◕ [M-212](#m-212) — Render the search term as plain text

**Classification:** Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-04](#tc-input-04)

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** An attacker sends a victim a link to /#`/search?q=&lt`;iframe src="javascript:`alert(document.cookie)`"&gt;. `filterTable` reads q, marks it as trusted HTML and binds it to [`innerHTML`]. The script runs in the app origin and can read the JWT from `localStorage`.

```typescript
→ 143 │       this.searchValue = this.sanitizer.bypassSecurityTrustHtml(queryParam) // vuln-code-snippet vuln-line localXssChallenge xssBonusChallenge

```

<a id="t-045"></a><a id="f-045"></a>
#### F-045 · Reflected XSS

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Angular Client Application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/track-result/track-result.component.ts:48`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** An attacker sends a victim a track-result link whose id parameter holds an iframe javascript: payload. When the reflected XSS challenge branch is enabled, the server echoes the ID, cut to 60 characters, as `orderId`.

The client wraps it in &lt;code&gt; and bypasses sanitization, so the script runs in the victim's session.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

